# Skills · C · part 42

857 entries, cell-type-differential-abundance-testing to centro-computacion. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cell-type-differential-abundance-testing**: Use when a samples-by-features LC/MS intensity matrix (raw or batch-corrected) has group labels and the goal is to rank or count metabolomic features that differ between groups at a relaxed FDR threshold such as ≤ 0.2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cell-type-differential-abundance-testing))
- **cell-type-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when a single-cell dataset contains annotated cell types and two conditions, and you need per-cell-type DE gene counts and magnitudes to rank the biological response across populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-differential-expression-analysis))
- **cell-type-disjoint-cross-validation-splitting**: Use when benchmarking cross-modality translation methods (e.g., scRNA-seq to scATAC-seq) for generalization to cell types absent from training, mimicking the MB/MCC/MK/PBMC unseen-cell-type evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-disjoint-cross-validation-splitting))
- **cell-type-drug-response-ranking**: Use when you need to identify which cell type in an untreated disease scRNA-seq dataset is most responsive to a drug with a known target gene, without paired treated/untreated samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-drug-response-ranking))
- **cell-type-enrichment-analysis**: Use when SINCERA hierarchical clustering has produced cell clusters and each cluster must be annotated with a predicted cell type using differentially expressed genes and a Fisher's exact test against the EBI Expression Atlas annotation table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-enrichment-analysis))
- **cell-type-entropy-coefficient-of-variation-benchmarking**: Use when comparing segmentation methods on spatial transcriptomics data (e.g., MERSCOPE melanoma, CosMx lung) where no pixel-level ground truth exists and you need reference-based homogeneity vs cell-size consistency metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-entropy-coefficient-of-variation-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-epiallele-trajectory-comparison**: Use when comparing methylated-core/epiallele structures across purified cell populations (e.g., astrocytes, neurons, MOEs from cerebellum and cortex) to test for shared precursor cores during postnatal differentiation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-epiallele-trajectory-comparison))
- **cell-type-expression-aggregation**: Use when a scRNA-seq study stored as an AnnData/H5AD object must be loaded into a relational sparse expression table and queried for gene-level per-cell-type averages, e.g. to replicate Cellenium's server-side aggregation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-expression-aggregation))
- **cell-type-expression-specificity-ranking**: Use when a query gene of interest (e.g. 'insulin') must be matched to the annotated cell types that specifically express it, within one study or across multiple ingested scRNA-seq studies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-expression-specificity-ranking))
- **cell-type-f1-score-computation**: Use when comparing label-transfer accuracy of scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq integration methods on cells with held-out ground-truth annotations, especially under varying annotation budgets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-f1-score-computation))
- **cell-type-flux-differential-testing**: Use when per-cell metabolic fluxes have been predicted from scRNA-seq (scFEA) and must be compared across reported cell-type labels (e.g. cancer, myeloid, T cells) to test which population has the highest glucose metabolic rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cell-type-flux-differential-testing))
- **cell-type-fraction-heatmap-visualization**: Use when bulk RNA-seq samples have been deconvolved against a single-cell reference (e.g., via scPlant decomposeBulk on an annotated Seurat object) and you need a heatmap of per-sample cell-type fractions to compare compositions across conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-fraction-heatmap-visualization))
- **cell-type-functional-annotation**: Use when an unsupervised clustering (e.g., Leiden) of scRNA-seq data has produced clusters with no labels and you must assign a cell type from the functional enrichment of cluster-specific genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-functional-annotation))
- **cell-type-gene-signature-definition**: Use when cell types have been annotated across merged datasets (e.g., after Harmony-kernel integration and label propagation) and a unified marker-gene signature per type/subtype is needed, such as neuronal (LINGO2, CUX2, SST) and glial (SLC1A2, MBP, CLDN5) markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-gene-signature-definition))
- **cell-type-ground-truth-precision-evaluation**: Use when a real scRNA-seq dataset has trusted cell-type labels serving as ground truth and you need to quantify how precisely a gene-set or pathway score (TRS, scDRS) retrieves the trait-relevant cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-ground-truth-precision-evaluation))
- **cell-type-held-out-benchmarking**: Use when benchmarking scRNA-seq integration methods on two datasets with known cell-type compositions and you want to verify that a cell type present in only one dataset is left unmatched instead of incorrectly aligned. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-held-out-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-hierarchy-construction**: Use when training a custom Census cell-type annotation model from a scRNA-seq reference (e.g. Tabula Sapiens plus CCLE) that requires inferring the cell-type hierarchy and fitting node classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-hierarchy-construction))
- **cell-type-homology-annotation**: Use when integrating single-cell datasets from multiple species whose cell type annotations use different or dataset-specific ontology terms, and a shared cell type homology mapping is required before integration and scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-homology-annotation))
- **cell-type-hop-distance-computation**: Use when you have an annotated SpatialData object (cell boundaries, cell-type labels, niche assignments) and need to test whether specific cell populations exclusively interact with themselves or are spatially restricted to particular niches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cell-type-hop-distance-computation))
- **cell-type-identification-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether joint (all-slices) inference improves cell type labeling accuracy of spatial transcriptomics methods, or when comparing ST cell type identification methods on simulated Visium data with known ground-truth single-cell labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-identification-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-identification-performance-metrics**: Use when comparing or tuning a scRNA-seq cell type identifier on annotated datasets and you need to quantify correct/erroneous assignments for known and unknown cell populations (C, E, EA, CUA, EUA). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-identification-performance-metrics))
- **cell-type-identification-potts-model-map-inference**: Use when you need per-cell (not per-spot) discrete cell type labels for ST spots (Visium, Slide-seq), given nucleus segmentation outputs and a labeled scRNA-seq reference, with spatial smoothness enforced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-identification-potts-model-map-inference))
- **cell-type-importance-visualization**: Use when you have dsCellNet-inferred ligand-receptor networks across multiple developmental time points (e.g., GSE153164 corticogenesis stages) and need per-cell-type incoming/outgoing/total edge counts to rank the most communicating cell types per stage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-importance-visualization))
- **cell-type-interaction-overlap-statistics**: Use when gABC/ABC-scored enhancer-gene interactions have been predicted per cell type from snATAC-seq candidate enhancers and you need per-cell-type interaction counts plus cross-cell-type overlap/shared fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-interaction-overlap-statistics))
- **cell-type-isoform-usage-aggregation**: Use when SingleCellExperiment 3'UTR isoform count matrices (*.txs.Rds, *.genes.Rds) from scUTRquant must be summarized per cell type and genes labeled multi-UTR vs IPA under a ≥10% isoform-fraction cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-isoform-usage-aggregation))
- **cell-type-isoform-usage-analysis**: Use when single-cell PacBio Iso-Seq data must be compared across cell types to identify which isoforms (e.g., ENST transcripts) dominate per cell type, or to test whether a gene undergoes major isoform switching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cell-type-isoform-usage-analysis))
- **cell-type-label-alignment-evaluation**: Use when you have per-cell embeddings (e.g., tGPT transformer features) plus ground-truth cell type annotations and must quantify clustering alignment via NMI, ARI, and FMI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-alignment-evaluation))
- **cell-type-label-guided-bayesian-matrix-factorization**: Use when scRNA-seq count matrices come with trusted cell type labels and the goal is dropout imputation that leverages those labels, e.g. benchmarking silhouette/Pearson clustering metrics against scImpute, DrImpute, PBLR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-guided-bayesian-matrix-factorization))
- **cell-type-label-hierarchy-classification**: Use when benchmarking marker gene panels against a two-layer cell-type label hierarchy on scRNA-seq data, or when testing whether fewer hierarchy-aware markers match a one-vs-all baseline panel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-hierarchy-classification))
- **cell-type-label-permutation-ablation**: Use when benchmarking NeuronChat's projection prediction on retrograde-labeling ground truth and you must rule out that benchmark performance is driven by node cell-type labels rather than the specific ground-truth graph topology. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-permutation-ablation))
- **cell-type-label-permutation-testing**: Use when constructing a cell–cell communication network from scRNA-seq data and you need to decide which sender–receiver L–R interactions are statistically significant rather than keeping all expression-product edges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-permutation-testing))
- **cell-type-label-prediction**: Use when annotating cell types in scRNA-seq data without training on the test dataset, or when benchmarking a zero-shot label predictor against supervised classifiers like CellTypist on a multi-batch atlas such as the open-problems pancreas dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-prediction))
- **cell-type-label-propagation-across-datasets**: Use when multiple independent scRNA-seq datasets (e.g., three cortex studies totaling ~105,099 cells) are jointly integrated and one or more reference annotations must be propagated to unlabeled cells without re-clustering from scratch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-propagation-across-datasets))
- **cell-type-label-supervised-feature-ranking**: Use when building a Cellcano reference for scATAC-seq celltyping: the reference gene score matrix (from ArchR) has annotated cell type labels and features must be reduced to cell-type-specific genes before MLP training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-label-supervised-feature-ranking))
- **cell-type-marker-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a cell-type-specific gene network (weighted edge matrix) for one of 26 cell types and need to verify that its node scores recover the correct pangaloDB cell-type markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-enrichment-analysis))
- **cell-type-marker-enrichment-annotation**: Use when scQA clustering (labels.txt) has assigned cells to clusters and you must confirm each cluster's key genes overlap known markers, hub genes, and DEGs (e.g., endothelial ADTRP, CDH5, CLEC14A, ACVRL1). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-enrichment-annotation))
- **cell-type-marker-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when you have a gene marker list selected from scRNA-seq phenotypes and need to verify it recovers the correct tissues and cell types by enrichment against a curated reference set such as HuBMAP_ASCTplusB_augmented_2022. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-enrichment-interpretation))
- **cell-type-marker-gene-matrix-handling**: Use when reconstructing or fine-tuning the DCNet network and you need to wire 9078 marker genes to 434 cell-type hidden neurons via the zero-one relation matrix and align input/output neuron ordering with index.pkl. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-gene-matrix-handling))
- **cell-type-marker-gene-selection**: Use when you have a raw/normalized scRNA-seq count matrix paired with manually annotated cell-type labels and need a per-cell-type positive marker gene list to drive marker-based or marker-learning cell-type prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-gene-selection))
- **cell-type-marker-gene-validation**: Use when scBridge (or a baseline such as scJoint, Seurat or GLUE) has produced predicted cell-type labels on target scATAC/ADT data and you need marker-based evidence that the predicted populations (e.g. Naive vs Effector CD4+ T cells) are biologically correct. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-gene-validation))
- **cell-type-marker-gmv-inference**: Use when you have an annotated single-cell dataset (e.g., the Field et al. week-2 cortical organoid data, GEO GSE106245) plus a cell type marker GMT file and want interpretable, module-level per-cell cell type activity rather than opaque clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-gmv-inference))
- **cell-type-marker-validation**: Use when a single-cell TE matrix (scTE .h5ad output from scRNA-seq or scTEATAC output from scATAC-seq) has been clustered and you must confirm cell-type assignments or marker-TE specificity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-validation))
- **cell-type-marker-verification-from-loadings**: Use when a scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g. SingleCellExperiment) has been embedded with correspondence analysis and you want to check whether high-loading genes correspond to known cell-type markers (e.g. GNLY, TYROBP, LYZ, S100A8, CD74, HLA-DRA) and interpret cell-population associations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-marker-verification-from-loadings))
- **cell-type-matching-metric-evaluation**: Use when comparing cross-species cell type matching pipelines (e.g., human vs mouse vs marmoset primary motor cortex scRNA-seq) and you need reproducible ADS/recall metrics, including gene-matching ablations that test whether ProtBERT gene matches outperform BLAST matches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-matching-metric-evaluation))
- **cell-type-mean-expression-profile-computation**: Use when preparing labelled scRNA-seq training data (e.g. SingleCellExperiment objects with known cell labels) and you need per-cell-type mean expression profiles plus a cross-cell-type correlation matrix to decide a classifier strategy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-mean-expression-profile-computation))
- **cell-type-neighborhood-enrichment-analysis**: Use when single-cell data have been pseudo-spatially mapped onto ST spots (e.g., via STEM's netsc2st mapping) and the goal is to quantify spatial proximity/colocalization between rare cell types such as TSKs, endothelial cells, or pDCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-neighborhood-enrichment-analysis))
- **cell-type-neighbourhood-proportion-computation**: Use when comparing the fidelity of cell-to-cell neighbourhood (cell-cell association) structure between two spatial transcriptomics representations — e.g. bin2cell-reconstructed cells vs default 8 μm Visium HD bins — against an expert-annotated MERFISH reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cell-type-neighbourhood-proportion-computation))
- **cell-type-nomenclature-standardization**: Use when combining marker gene sets from multiple published databases or cell-sorting sources whose gene symbols and cell type labels follow non-standard, inconsistent nomenclature, and you need harmonized, comparable sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-nomenclature-standardization))
- **cell-type-perturbation-detection**: Use when you have a normalized scRNA-seq count matrix with per-cell metadata assigning condition, patient, and cluster, and need to identify which predefined cell types are transcriptomically perturbed between two conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-perturbation-detection))
- **cell-type-perturbation-ranking**: Use when you have untreated diseased scRNA-seq data with cell-type annotations and a known drug target gene, and need to identify which cell types are drug-responsive or resistant without paired treated/untreated samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-perturbation-ranking))
- **cell-type-phenotype-association-testing**: Use when cell-type proportions have been estimated from bulk RNA-seq (via BisqueRNA bulk.props or BisqueMarker) and you need to test associations with phenotypes such as BMI, Matsuda index, or Braak stage, replicating reported cell-composition associations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-phenotype-association-testing))
- **cell-type-polygenic-regulome**: Tier C index only (human study): do not use it to answer a person's own health question. Ma et al., Nature Aging 2026: scMORE combines single-cell multiomics with GWAS to find cell-type-specific regulons for immune- and ageing-related diseases. Holds the published TRS formula. Use when someone asks about this study, polygenic single-cell regulomes, scMORE or eRegulons; not for a person's own measurements. ([zwbao/longevity-skills](https://github.com/zwbao/longevity-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-type-polygenic-regulome) · ★1)
- **cell-type-positive-fraction-quantification**: Use when single-cell AnnData atlases spanning multiple ages (e.g., Tabula Muris Senis, 328K cells, 19 tissues, 1-30 months) must be scored for senescence and aggregated into per-cell-type positive-cell proportions or old/young ratios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-positive-fraction-quantification))
- **cell-type-potency-ranking-analysis**: Use when a scRNA-Seq count matrix with malignant/non-malignant or cell-type annotations is available and the goal is to estimate and rank differentiation potency of cell types without marker genes, feature selection, or training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-potency-ranking-analysis))
- **cell-type-prediction-confusion-matrix-computation**: Use when you have Cellcano (or similar supervised) predicted cell-type labels for a target scATAC-seq dataset plus ground-truth labels (e.g., FACS-sorted FACS labels embedded in cell identifiers) and need to quantify per-class prediction accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-prediction-confusion-matrix-computation))
- **cell-type-probability-classification**: Use when single-cell expression data has a small labeled training set and you need probabilistic cell type predictions plus predictive entropy to guide cell selection or evaluate annotation uncertainty. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-probability-classification))
- **cell-type-probability-expression-correlation**: Use when you need marker genes for cell types absent from training data, given OnClass prediction matrices (pred_Y_all.released.npy) and single-cell expression data such as Tabula Muris Senis FACS cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-probability-expression-correlation))
- **cell-type-profile-matching**: Use when ST pixels are cell mixtures and you need to assign reference-free deconvolved cell-type transcriptional profiles to known cell populations and compare them across conditions (e.g., aged vs young) for perturbation-driven gene changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-profile-matching))
- **cell-type-proportion-accuracy-evaluation**: Decision support for validating false-positive control of Scanpro proportion analysis on null simulations, and for choosing pseudo-replicate counts (2–14) based on total cell counts per condition (~1,000 to ~50,000 cells). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-proportion-accuracy-evaluation))
- **cell-type-proportion-computation-per-condition**: Use when comparing cell-type composition of an AI-generated (semi-profiled) cohort against real-profiled single-cell ground truth across defined conditions, e.g. COVID-19 severity states (Healthy, Asymptomatic, Mild, Moderate, Severe, Critical). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-proportion-computation-per-condition))
- **cell-type-proportion-controlled-synthetic-data-generation**: Use when you need a simulated scRNA-seq count matrix with known ground-truth cell-type labels and controlled per-type cell numbers — e.g. to benchmark clustering methods on a rare cell type (&lt;5% of cells). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-proportion-controlled-synthetic-data-generation))
- **cell-type-proportion-correlation-analysis**: Use when evaluating SC-ST mapping outputs (SC-ST / ST-SC mapping matrices) against ground-truth ST cell-type labels, to quantify whether each method preserves cell-type composition of spots rather than just coordinate accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-proportion-correlation-analysis))
- **cell-type-proportion-correlation-evaluation**: Use when a decomposition method's estimated cell-type proportions (e.g., BisqueRNA's res$bulk.props) must be scored against known or reference proportions per individual and cell type using Pearson correlation and RMSD. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-proportion-correlation-evaluation))
- **cell-type-proportion-quantification**: Use when you have raw scRNA-seq UMI counts from a heterogeneous cell suspension (e.g. 68k PBMCs) and need cluster-level cell-type composition percentages validated against a published population breakdown. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-proportion-quantification))
- **cell-type-proportion-statistical-testing**: Use when single-cell resolution data (AnnData with sample, cluster, condition metadata columns) needs differential compositional analysis of cell-type proportions, with or without biological replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-proportion-statistical-testing))
- **cell-type-proportion-summarization**: Use when per-cell DA scores (or similar per-cell condition-association statistics) have been computed on an integrated single-cell dataset with cell-type annotations and you need to rank cell types by the fraction of cells linked to a condition such as severe disease. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-proportion-summarization))
- **cell-type-protein-profiling-integration**: Use when you have co-registered IMC (protein imaging mass cytometry) and SIMS (secondary ion mass spectrometry for metabolites) data from the same tissue regions, cell segmentation masks, and need to assign cell types based on protein expression patterns, then overlay those assignments onto. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-protein-profiling-integration) · 2 copies)
- **cell-type-purity-evaluation**: Use when a joint latent embedding (e.g. scConfluence latent in mdata.obsm['latent']) must be quantitatively validated against known cell-type labels, as in diagonal-integration benchmarks or hyperparameter sensitivity sweeps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-purity-evaluation))
- **cell-type-rank-dimension-reduction-visualization**: Use when a scRank analysis has produced a ranked list of drug-responsive cell types (obj@cell_type_rank) and you need to check or display how the ranking distributes across single cells on a dimension-reduction embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-rank-dimension-reduction-visualization))
- **cell-type-recovery-assessment**: Use when you have simulated scRNA-seq count data with known cell-type labels (e.g., from sc_mosim/SPARSim via MOSim) and need to quantify how well a clustering tool recovers the simulated cell populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cell-type-recovery-assessment))
- **cell-type-recovery-assessment-across-graining-levels**: Use when selecting a graining level for metacell construction and you need to check that rare cell populations are not merged into other types, especially in datasets of varying complexity or size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-recovery-assessment-across-graining-levels))
- **cell-type-recovery-auc-evaluation**: Use when comparing pathway activity scoring methods on scRNA-seq data where ground-truth cell labels exist (e.g., SERGIO simulations or annotated datasets), to measure cell-type recovery AUC and accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-recovery-auc-evaluation))
- **cell-type-rejection-thresholding**: Use when annotating single-cell RNA-seq cells via marker counting and you must conservatively reject low-confidence assignments (possible unknown cell types) before downstream cluster reassignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-rejection-thresholding))
- **cell-type-relationship-tree-inference**: Use when you need to assess whether a subsampled ('sketched') single-cell dataset preserves cross-study cell-type subtype relationships, by harmonizing labels with CellHint and comparing the inferred relationship tree against a full-data reference tree. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-relationship-tree-inference))
- **cell-type-relative-interaction-quantification**: Use when several pre-computed cell-cell communication (CCI) datasets from different conditions must be compared cell-type-wise, to test whether a cell type (e.g., mast cells or NKT-p) consistently mediates more interactions in one condition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-relative-interaction-quantification))
- **cell-type-replicability-subsampling-simulation**: Use when you must stress-test whether a cross-dataset cell-type identification method still recovers a target type that is rare (1–20% of cells) and only subtly different from the out-group, rather than well-separated abundant types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-replicability-subsampling-simulation))
- **cell-type-scaling-benchmark-analysis**: Use when evaluating whether a transcriptomic perturbation score (e.g., number of DEGs, scDist distance) scales with the number of cells per cell type in single-cell RNA-seq data, or when benchmarking speed of differential-state methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-scaling-benchmark-analysis))
- **cell-type-scatter-plot-visualization**: Use when a small marker-gene panel has been selected and you need a visual check that the reduced-dimensional projection separates known cell type labels into distinct clusters, e.g., reproducing a published t-SNE of cells projected onto 15 markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-scatter-plot-visualization))
- **cell-type-score-aggregation-across-clusters**: Use when you have a cell-type-by-cell score matrix (es.max) from sctype_score() and Seurat cluster identities and must assign a single label per cluster, with an Unknown fallback for low-confidence assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-score-aggregation-across-clusters))
- **cell-type-score-computation**: Use when you have EpiRegio GeneQuery REM tables (regressionCoefficient, per-cell-type DNase1 signal) for a gene and need cell type-specific REM contributions to gene expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-score-computation))
- **cell-type-score-computation**: Use when a scRNA-seq analysis requires scoring cells against known cell-type markers (e.g., producing per-cell scores/labels for MASI-based annotation or for pipeline variants in integration benchmarks). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-score-computation))
- **cell-type-score-ranking-of-rems**: Use when a set of REMs from an EpiRegio Gene Query must be prioritized by cell type/tissue specificity (e.g. 'heart') before downstream TF motif enrichment such as PASTAA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-score-ranking-of-rems))
- **cell-type-score-space-centroid-distance-analysis**: Use when transferring labels from a reference atlas with MASI and query data may contain cell types not present in the reference: cells whose closest-centroid distance approaches their farthest-centroid distance (ratio near 1, certainty near 0) should be flagged as unassigned. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-score-space-centroid-distance-analysis))
- **cell-type-separation-visualization**: Use when a VEGA (or similar interpretable VAE) model has been trained on single-cell RNA-seq with gene-module-wired latents and you must check that latent dimensions separate annotated cell populations before interpreting module activities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-separation-visualization))
- **cell-type-signature-deconvolution**: Use when you need to infer relative cell type composition from bulk RNA-seq by scoring curated cell-type gene signatures (GMT module lists) with sample-level PC1 module activity rather than a regression-based deconvolution tool. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-signature-deconvolution))
- **cell-type-silhouette-and-batch-entropy-metrics**: Use when comparing cell-type annotation or integration methods on the same labeled multi-batch scRNA-seq datasets and you need standardized scores (silhouette, batch entropy, F1, accuracy) plus statistical significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-silhouette-and-batch-entropy-metrics))
- **cell-type-similarity-quartile-stratification**: Use when evaluating BIRD-predicted differential DH between many cell-type pairs and you need to know how prediction–truth correlation (Pearson, all loci vs differential loci) depends on the similarity of the two cell types' DH profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-similarity-quartile-stratification))
- **cell-type-spatial-distance-quantification**: Use when scRNA-seq cells have been projected into a spatially informed pseudo-space and you need to test whether the spatial proximity between annotated cell types differs between biological conditions (e.g., normal vs. disease). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-spatial-distance-quantification))
- **cell-type-specific-co-expression-inference**: Use when raw UMI-count scRNA-seq data need cell-type-specific co-expression networks, when correlation-based methods give inflated false positives due to sequencing depth confounding, or when differential co-expression between disease groups is sought. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-co-expression-inference))
- **cell-type-specific-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data (e.g. BALF or PBMC count matrices from GEO) has been clustered and cell-type-annotated, and the goal is to test candidate genes (e.g. MCEMP1, HLA-DRA, ETS1) for differential expression between severity groups per cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-differential-expression-analysis))
- **cell-type-specific-differential-peak-analysis**: Use when single-cell CC/qbed insertion data (e.g., Brd4 mouse cortex, GSE148448) must be converted to a cell-by-peak AnnData and differential TF binding peaks per cell type must be linked to nearby gene expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-differential-peak-analysis))
- **cell-type-specific-enhancer-motif-ranking**: Use when you have trained BOM/XGBoost classifiers on cell-type-specific distal regulatory elements (e.g. mouse E8.25 scATAC-derived CREs) and need per-motif SHAP importance rankings plus local explanations at a locus of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-enhancer-motif-ranking))
- **cell-type-specific-gene-expression-identification**: Use when single-cell expression data for known cell types (e.g., Astro, MG, L2/3, Vip) and per-cell nested TLD boundary calls are available and you need to designate genes or boundaries as cell-type-specific. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-gene-expression-identification))
- **cell-type-specific-gene-thresholding**: Use when running deCS.fisher on a marker gene list or GWAS trait-associated gene list and you must first convert a cell type specificity score panel into discrete per-cell-type gene sets via a score cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-gene-thresholding))
- **cell-type-specific-genomic-data-integration**: Use when scoring structural variants (inversions, translocations, deletions, duplications) with hg19 breakpoints against phenotypes like head_neck, limbs, neurodevelopmental, cardiovascular, vision_eye, liver_biliary_system or pituitary to identify causative genes and coding vs long-range. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-genomic-data-integration))
- **cell-type-specific-ipa-usage-comparison**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq alignments and InPACT-derived IPA usage estimates are available and the goal is to flag IPA events whose usage is restricted to one cell type versus others. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-ipa-usage-comparison))
- **cell-type-specific-locus-annotation**: Use when DA peaks or age-related expression changes from PBMC ATAC-seq/RNA-seq must be assigned to cell-type-specific loci to test whether aging signatures (e.g., B cell decline) are shared or sex-specific between men and women. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-locus-annotation))
- **cell-type-specific-loop-summit-annotation**: Use when scHi-C-derived loops from matched cell type subsets (e.g. 261-cell astrocyte/microglia/ODC/L2/3 neuron SnapHiC runs) need epigenomic and expression validation against matched bulk references. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-loop-summit-annotation))
- **cell-type-specific-model-validation**: Use when a model trained on paired bulk omics data (e.g., TCGA miRNA-mRNA) must be validated for cell-type-specific inference against scRNA-seq data with known cell-type miRNA profiles (e.g., Isakova Kidney-Breast-Skin GSE151334). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-model-validation))
- **cell-type-specific-network-annotation**: Use when you have cell-type-specific gene/GRN networks (edge-score matrices per cell type) and need to validate biological relevance via TF, cell-type-marker, and ENCODE TF-target enrichment before benchmarking inference methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-network-annotation))
- **cell-type-specific-pseudobulk-mixed-model-fitting**: Use when per-individual, per-cell-type scRNA-seq data must be aggregated into CTP/ctnu/P pseudobulk inputs and fit with CTMM linear mixed models to quantify or test cell type-specific interindividual variance (and its differentiation between cell types/stages). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-pseudobulk-mixed-model-fitting))
- **cell-type-specific-regression-metrics-evaluation**: Use when predicted and true count matrices must be scored per cell type for multiple prediction scenarios (within-batch/cross-batch, in-sample/out-of-sample) and compared against baselines like scGen and scPreGAN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-regression-metrics-evaluation))
- **cell-type-specific-regulatory-landscape-integration**: Use when interpreting patient structural variants (inversions, deletions, duplications, translocations) whose pathogenic effect is suspected to be non-coding/long-range, and cell type-specific enhancer and TAD data are needed to nominate disease genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-regulatory-landscape-integration))
- **cell-type-specific-regulatory-variant-mapping**: Use when mapping enhancer-resident noncoding GWAS SNPs to putative target genes via cell type-specific chromatin loops (SnapHiC *.postprocessed.summits.bedpe), e.g. neuropsychiatric SNPs in human prefrontal cortex. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-regulatory-variant-mapping))
- **cell-type-specific-rem-filtering**: Use when submitting a gene list to EpiRegio's Gene Query and you need REMs restricted to a relevant cell type or tissue context rather than genome-wide REMs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-rem-filtering))
- **cell-type-specific-spatial-de-testing**: Use when the goal is to identify genes whose spatial expression varies within a specific cell type — e.g. testing whether imputation of non-MERFISH genes into a MERFISH slice increases detectable cell-type-specific spatially DE genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-spatial-de-testing))
- **cell-type-specific-variance-component-estimation**: Use when scRNA-seq counts with cell and individual metadata need to be summarized into cell type-specific pseudobulk and tested for genes with significant cell type-specific interindividual expression variance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specific-variance-component-estimation))
- **cell-type-specificity-reference-panel-matching**: Use when a preprocessed, z-scored cluster-level marker-gene expression matrix (clusters as columns) needs automatic cell type labels matched against a human cell type reference panel such as MonacoImmune or BlueprintEncode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specificity-reference-panel-matching))
- **cell-type-specificity-score-implementation**: Use when annotating clusters of a processed scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., Seurat object with scaled matrix) against a tissue-restricted marker database using both positive and negative markers, or when verifying a from-scratch reimplementation of the ScType scoring algorithm. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specificity-score-implementation))
- **cell-type-specificity-tscore-estimation**: Use when building or verifying deCS CTGene reference panels: you need per-cell-type ranked gene lists derived from bulk (t-score) or single-cell (z-score) specificity scores before annotation or enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-specificity-tscore-estimation))
- **cell-type-stratified-accuracy-comparison**: Use when you have an integration-adjusted expression matrix plus raw logcounts, consensus cell-type labels and per-sample severity metadata, and need to quantify whether integration improves cell-type-stratified prediction accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-stratified-accuracy-comparison))
- **cell-type-stratified-integration-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking scRNA-seq integration methods on datasets with imbalanced cell type proportions, where a cell type-aware batch-mixing metric (rather than plain iLISI) is needed to score batch mixing per cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-stratified-integration-evaluation))
- **cell-type-stratified-prediction-assessment**: Use when predicting held-out condition/batch scRNA-seq matrices (e.g., PW034-705) and you must judge prediction accuracy separately per cell type under both condition-only and condition-plus-batch scenarios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-stratified-prediction-assessment))
- **cell-type-stratified-pvalue-aggregation**: Use when a per-sample EMBEDR p-value sweep across kEff/perplexity exists for an annotated single-cell dataset and you need to determine whether cell types are embedded at different neighborhood scales. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-stratified-pvalue-aggregation))
- **cell-type-subpopulation-stratification**: Use when simulating perturbed trajectories from a differentiation time series and you need fate-outcome shifts computed per starting cell state rather than per whole timepoint. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-subpopulation-stratification))
- **cell-type-subset-analysis**: Use when benchmarking phase inference on heterogeneous droplet-based scRNA-seq where per-cell-type accuracy (e.g., median phase error per population) must be computed against known sampling times. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-subset-analysis))
- **cell-type-subset-cohort-analysis**: Use when analyzing droplet-based multi-subject single-cell count data where the goal is to test a cell-level predictor (like APOE expression) or subject-level genotype group within one annotated cell type, while accounting for hierarchical subject structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-subset-cohort-analysis))
- **cell-type-subsetting-from-scrna-seq**: Use when a scRNA-seq Seurat object or raw UMI count matrix contains mixed cell types and the analysis target is cell-type-specific co-expression inference or null-data simulation for one cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-subsetting-from-scrna-seq))
- **cell-type-supervised-classification**: Use when a labeled .h5ad scRNA-seq dataset needs per-cell informativeness scores (data values) for value-based subsampling, quality assessment, or identifying ambiguous/atypical cells relative to their annotated cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-supervised-classification))
- **cell-type-te-marker-identification**: Use when a locus-level TE quantification matrix (cell × TE counts) has been produced by MATES and the goal is to find TEs that mark specific cell populations recovered by clustering or by a gene-expression reference clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-te-marker-identification))
- **cell-type-term-mapping**: Use when scRNA-seq datasets carry heterogeneous free-text cell annotations (e.g., Tabula Muris Senis-style labels) that must be normalized to Cell Ontology terms before ontology-based classification or cross-dataset annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-term-mapping))
- **cell-type-text-embedding-similarity**: Use when you have Cell Ontology text embeddings (cl.ontology.nlp.emb), the ontology graph (cl.obo), and per-cell-type expression profiles, and you want to test whether ontology/text/expression similarity agree. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-text-embedding-similarity))
- **cell-type-tree-comparison-against-published-hierarchy**: Use when a learned or predicted cell-type hierarchy must be verified against a published cluster-correspondence figure, e.g. checking that AMB2016/AMB2018 visual cortex populations attach to the correct parents. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-tree-comparison-against-published-hierarchy))
- **cell-type-unbalancing-simulation**: Use when evaluating whether a single-cell diagonal integration method (scRNA with scATAC or surface protein) remains robust as cell populations become unbalanced or absent from one modality.
